Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [178] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAM-DQ是一种双四旋翼全驱动空中机械臂，能在0.447 kg质量下输出1.019 N·m扭矩，实现解耦6自由度控制，适用于高扭矩空中物理交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中物理交互要求飞行器在保持精确末端控制的同时产生大交互力和力矩。传统的欠驱动空中机械臂存在位置-姿态强耦合问题，而全驱动平台往往结构复杂、载荷有限、扭矩输出不足。因此，需要一种能够在高扭矩物理交互任务中实现解耦6自由度末端控制的高性能空中机械臂。

Method: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂。通过在中心框架两端通过被动关节安装两个四旋翼推进模块，并使用齿轮驱动的伺服电机调节指向方向，FAM-DQ实现了解耦的6自由度末端控制，具备全向操作能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 实验包括轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等。FAM-DQ实现最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比为2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过双四旋翼结构实现了高扭矩输出和解耦6自由度控制，适用于高扭矩空中物理交互任务，展现了其在复杂操作场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [179] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多种制造复杂软气动执行器的方法，发现FDM(熔融沉积建模)是最灵活的选择，其缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步改善，而其他方法各有明显限制。研究建立了一套实用的设计制造方法，为软体机器人应用中的复杂柔性气密执行器开发提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软气动执行器面临几何保真度、顺应性、结构完整性和气密性需要同时实现的挑战。目前缺乏系统性的制造方法评估，难以指导工程实践中的工艺选择。

Method: 通过过程筛选、基线制造、失效分析和过程改进四个阶段，系统评估了五种制造路线：热缩成型、硅胶铸造、粉末3D打印、液体3D打印和熔融沉积建模(FDM)，以区分固有的工艺限制与可纠正的制造缺陷。

Result: 各工艺存在明显局限：热缩成型受几何一致性限制；硅胶铸造受模具可及性和粘接界面影响；粉末基方法存在通道内残留材料问题；数字光处理受材料特性和后处理要求制约。FDM表现最佳，其缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步减少。研究还发现气密性不仅取决于壁厚，还与挤出路径架构相关，且当内部后处理受限时，无支撑几何结构至关重要。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一套实用的设计制造方法(DfM)，其中工艺选择由执行器架构与制造约束之间的兼容性指导。该方法为开发用于软体机器人应用的复杂、柔性和气密软气动执行器提供了实际指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [180] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量化、模块化的远程操作机器人主动碰撞避免框架，通过在末端执行器速度指令层进行操作，利用碰撞时间预测和Rodrigues旋转公式调整相对速度方向（保持大小不变）来避免碰撞，解决了传统人工势场法的死锁问题，并通过仿真和实物实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程操作机器人已能执行灵活的人臂类运动，但人工操作员在网络延迟或注意力有限的情况下，可能无法及时有效地避让突然出现的障碍物，存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出在末端执行器速度指令层操作的轻量化模块化主动碰撞避免框架：首先对点云进行预处理，然后基于带过冲保护的碰撞时间(TTC)预测潜在碰撞，最后使用Rodrigues旋转公式旋转相对速度向量，仅改变方向而保持大小不变，从而缓解传统人工势场(APF)方法的死锁问题。预测模块补偿了复杂远程操作流水线中点云处理延迟，轻量化设计满足远程操作所需的高频控制要求。

Result: 在多种场景下的仿真结果表明，该方法比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免率；在物理机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其碰撞避免效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量化模块化碰撞避免框架能够有效提升远程操作机器人的主动避障能力，通过速度方向调整策略解决了传统方法的局限，为远程操作安全提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [181] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NestDex框架，通过已学习的手部技能辅助演示收集，简化灵巧操作任务的演示采集过程，提升演示可靠性与效率，实现有效的自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作能够显著丰富机器人与物理世界的交互能力，但学习这些行为受限于收集一致且完整的任务演示的困难。与平行夹持操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作者在整个任务过程中协调臂部运动与精确的、富含接触的手指行为。

Method: 引入NestDex嵌套策略学习框架：操作者控制机械臂并通过单自由度离合器调节主动手部技能（而非直接指定完整手指轨迹）；内部手部策略根据最新本体感受历史调整运动，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活适当技能；由此产生的演示用于训练独立的外部视觉运动策略，在部署时不使用内部策略；手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间中的臂部命令。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示的可靠性和效率，经验评估结果支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: 该框架通过利用已学习的手部技能辅助演示收集，显著降低了灵巧操作任务演示采集的负担，并通过嵌套策略架构实现了高效、可靠的自主操作策略学习。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [182] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRE-VLA，一种面向自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的失败信息自演化框架。当采样轨迹全部较差时，组相对策略优化（GRPO）只能对失败排序而无法识别失败区域外的行为。FIRE-VLA利用低奖励、低多样性组触发自蒸馏，从同一模型的冻结副本中获取特权监督，无需外部大教师，从而在nuScenes数据集上降低平均L2误差和持续失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖组内奖励差异进行相对优化，但当所有轨迹均表现不佳时，该信号仅能排序失败而无法区分失败区域外的行为，限制了模型从失败中学习的能力。因此需要一种机制将未解决的失败转化为有效监督信号，引导策略向更优方向演化。

Method: FIRE-VLA在检测到低奖励、低多样性组时，从同一模型的冻结起始副本中触发同规模自蒸馏。教师模型可观测隐藏的未来轨迹，而学生模型仅基于已生成的前缀进行监督，监督范围限定在答案token。GRPO仍对所有组保持活跃。更新后的策略作为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化而改变，无需引入更大规模的外部教师。

Result: 基于相同的Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT检查点，在150个nuScenes场景的6019个样本上，FIRE-VLA在保持单样本规划能力相当的情况下，将G=4的平均L2误差从1.848米降至1.500米，并将评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。误差的降低主要来自罕见的严重失败轨迹，而非普通轨迹的均匀改善。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过失败信息自蒸馏有效利用了GRPO无法区分的失败区域外信息，实现了无需外部大教师的策略演化。该方法显著降低了自动驾驶VLA模型的平均误差和持续失败率，尤其改善了严重失败案例的表现，为强化学习在复杂轨迹优化中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [183] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于驱动-感知协同设计理念的一体化设计-建模-感知方法，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现电缆张力实时测量，并结合多体短厚梁模型并行计算框架和近端多轴力矩传感器，成功实现了卡盘驱动连续体手术机器人的实时尖端位姿估计、接触力和位置感知，无需占用紧凑的卡盘空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡盘驱动连续体手术机器人由于其紧凑型卡盘组件内部难以获取电缆张力信息，长期以来形状和力感知一直是发展瓶颈。如何在保持卡盘紧凑架构的前提下实现电缆张力反馈，是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 采用基于驱动-感知协同设计理念的一体化设计-建模-感知方法：1)在驱动系统电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，通过电缆反作用力下的微变形实现实时电缆张力测量；2)基于多体短厚梁模型提出并行计算框架，捕获短梁段剪切效应和多电缆协同相互作用，实现实时性能；3)结合近端多轴力矩传感器作为附加测量锚点；4)开发了单段和双段配置的卡盘驱动连续体手术机器人。

Result: 实验结果在单段和双段连续体机器人上验证了所提出框架的有效性，实现了实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力和位置感知。柔性元件成功实现了不占用紧凑卡盘空间的电缆张力测量。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过在不损害紧凑卡盘架构的情况下实现电缆张力反馈，使卡盘驱动连续体手术机器人的集成感知成为可能，为该类机器人的实际应用奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [184] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"刻意练习(DP)"主动技能学习算法，通过双线性规划精确计算预算最优的技能练习分配策略，使机器人在有限练习时间内最大化累积奖励。在长期操作任务的仿真和真实实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机器人在有限练习预算下学习顺序任务技能的问题。挑战在于计算预算最优分配需要对大量组合技能计划和庞大的练习预算进行推理，这是一个复杂的组合优化问题。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice (DP)主动技能学习算法，该方法估算掌握技能所需的时间和技能解锁的任务计划的累积奖励。核心贡献是一个双线性规划模型，能够使用现成的求解器精确计算预算最优分配。

Result: 在长期操作任务的仿真和真实世界实验中验证了DP算法的有效性，证明机器人能够最优地利用有限的练习时间获取有用策略并改进长期规划。

Conclusion: DP算法通过精确的双线性规划求解，实现了机器人在预算约束下的最优技能学习分配，解决了传统方法难以处理的组合优化挑战，为机器人自主学习提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [185] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Seeker方法，通过从动作中学习注意力机制，在无外部空间标签的情况下生成任务感知ROI，显著提升视觉运动学习的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROI接口依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别、可实施性标注）或使用动作派生裁剪（固定事件时机、代理点和裁剪尺度），当控制所需的视觉证据位于末端执行器之外或随任务进度连续变化时，这些裁剪会产生偏差。

Method: Seeker是一种任务和状态条件化读出机制，从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询并收集视觉证据，仅利用动作监督产生进程感知ROI。该ROI作为空间接口用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云滤波。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界测试中，Seeker在数据效率和鲁棒性方面优于无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪基线。在真实机器人上，平均域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker有效解决了现有ROI方法的局限性，通过从动作中学习动态、任务感知的兴趣区域，无需外部标签即可实现显著的数据效率提升和鲁棒性增强，为视觉运动学习提供了更优的空间接口解决方案。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [186] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactGuard 是一种预接触执行监控器，通过在隐视觉空间预测策略动作的短期后果，并在预测到可能失败时中止操作。模型在无标签机器人轨迹上训练，学习多视角视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；失败探测器在小量标签数据上训练。部署时，在接触前锚定预测，沿策略动作滚动模型，验证接触后隐状态。实验表明，ContactGuard 在真实接触丰富操作任务中比消融方法更准确地预测失败，且无需修改底层策略即可作为实时机器人预接触中止信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作失败往往只在机器人已经投入接触后才被检测到。这在腕部摄像头设置中尤其受限：近距离夹爪-物体视图有助于观察接触，但错误的接近方式可能已经在传统检测器反应前推动、错过、滑动或扰动物体。

Method: ContactGuard 预测策略计划动作块的短期后果，在隐视觉空间中进行，如果预测的未来隐状态表明可能失败，则中止。其隐世界模型从无标签机器人轨迹训练，以预测在计划动作下的紧凑多视角视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测。随后，一个轻量级失败探测器从小量标签的接触前片段训练。部署时，ContactGuard 在即将到来的接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下滚动模型，并验证预测的接触后隐状态。

Result: 在真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard 比直接和腐败动作消融方法更准确地预测失败，并且无需修改底层策略即可转移到实时机器人作为预接触中止信号。

Conclusion: ContactGuard 提供了一种有效的预接触失败预测系统，通过在可能失败时中止接触前的操作，提高了接触丰富操作任务的鲁棒性，且无需修改底层策略。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [187] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EDD框架，通过显式解耦环境和社会知识，利用重放式回演缓解遗忘，使社交机器人能在不同室内场景中持续学习并保留已获得的社交行为知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要在多样环境中运行，相同布置在不同场景下可能需要不同的社交行为（如拥挤家庭中发起对话可接受，而在办公室会议中会打扰）。由于无法预先预知所有规范和环境，机器人需要持续学习以适应经验并保持已有知识。先前工作未解决领域增量场景问题，即机器人逐步遇到不同上下文，其中环境和社会线索共同决定行为的适当性。

Method: 本文提出显式解耦双分支(EDD)框架，明确分离环境相关知识和社交智能体相关知识，使用基于重放的回演技术缓解遗忘，学习机器人在多个室内领域中行为的适当性（如清洁、服务、发起对话）。

Result: 实验表明EDD优于多个最先进基线，消融研究进一步评估了不同解耦策略和对领域顺序的敏感性。代码已公开于https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git。

Conclusion: EDD框架成功填补了领域增量持续学习研究的空白，通过显式解耦机制使机器人能够在不同社交环境中有效学习并保留先前知识，实现了对环境和社会线索的联合建模。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [188] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法探查视觉-语言-动作模型的残余流，发现任务进度（归一化剩余时间）可从激活值中线性读出。该信号在预训练PaliGemma主干网络中已存在，且单一线性探针可泛化至未见任务，可作为部署VLA的有效监控工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型正快速向通用操作策略部署发展，但目前缺乏理解这些模型内部表示或进行运行时监控的基础工具，急需可解释的方法来检测已部署VLAs的运行状态。

Method: 借鉴机制可解释性思想，对π₀.₅模型的残余流进行探针分析，检查任务进度信号是否存在于预训练PaliGemma主干中。训练单一线性探针，测试其对未见任务的泛化能力以及在多提示数据下的语言反事实变化，并将其用作无标签的OOD检测器。

Result: 任务进度信号在预训练主干网络中即已存在，早于机器人特定数据训练。线性探针可泛化至未见任务并对语言反事实产生变化，但无法实现有效的策略控制。作为OOD检测器时，能检测停滞的任务进度，与最先进方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: VLA具有丰富且可线性读取的内部表示，能够编码任务进度等语义量。学习读取这些信号为监控已部署视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量、可解释的途径，有助于实际应用中的模型监测。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [189] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于浏览器的数字化测试场，用于评估海洋滑翔机路径规划算法，通过OSSE实验验证了5个经典规划器在54次任务中的表现，并实现了GliderFlight 1.2.0的浏览器原生运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复原位评估面临车辆、操作员、部署回收资源稀缺以及海洋条件无法重置的挑战，需要一种可重现、标准化的测试环境。

Method: 开发了一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字化测试场，将选定区域转化为可重现的四维实验环境。系统引导用户完成区域选择、地形建模、时空深度驱动、科学目标设定、任务分解、路径指定、洋流执行、观测生成和评分等流程。该系统建立了统一的计划到观测契约，整合了载具、传感和评估器假设，支持手动路线、内置算法和导入的经典或学习型规划器输出。

Result: 在受控OSSE实验中，对5个经典规划器进行了评估（2个场景、3个确定性种子、60小时校准窗口），54次任务全部完成并安全回收，无硬性违规。规划器排名和潜水策略效果揭示了运营-科学权衡。公开部署提供了现场参考审计以界定当前运动学边界。GliderFlight 1.2.0通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现了浏览器原生运行，达到了与原生版本相同的功能水平。

Conclusion: 该数字化测试场为任务规模的前部署实验提供了科学可追溯性和组件合格认证，建立了用于海洋滑翔机规划算法评估的高保真多层次模拟路径，为资源稀缺条件下的标准化评估提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [190] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanTracker与HumanScore为人类运动追踪提供感知对齐的评估框架，包含153小时专业表演数据，能揭示运动学指标忽略的接触与稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法与人类感知不一致：运动学误差仅平均帧间姿态差异，忽略不稳定支撑和错误接触等关键物理缺陷（如脚部滑行、时机错误的触地）；现有测试集规模小且缺乏多样性，无法充分测试接触密集、长时间行为。

Method: 提出HumanTracker基准：包含约153小时专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四个运动家族并提供文本标签用于细粒度诊断。同时提出HumanScore：基于12K运动对（24K运动）训练的偏好对齐指标。

Result: 跨代表性最先进追踪器测试，HumanScore能更好预测人类偏好，揭示运动学指标经常忽略的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker使人类运动追踪评估同时实现感知对齐和可扩展性，通过大规模多样数据集和偏好对齐指标解决现有评估方法的局限性。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [DualPI2 Active Queue Management in ns-3: Implementation And Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12513)
*Maria Eduarda Veras,Eduardo Freitas,Assis T. de Oliveira Filho,Djamel Sadok,Judith Kelner*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究在ns-3中实现了高保真的DualPI2 AQM模型，通过25种BDP场景的物理测试验证，为L4S网络研究提供了可靠的仿真工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低延迟应用需求推动L4S标准发展，其核心组件DualPI2 AQM确保可缩放流量与经典流量的安全共存。尽管L4S采用率增长，但ns-3模拟器缺乏高保真、最新的DualPI2模型，限制了研究社区对L4S性能的评估能力。

Method: 在ns-3中实现综合的DualPI2 AQM，镜像官方Linux内核架构。实现包括之前仿真中缺失的代表性机制：基于信用的加权轮询(WRR)调度、逐步标记和过载保护。通过与物理Linux测试床进行对比验证，涵盖25种不同的带宽延迟乘积(BDP)场景。

Result: 实验结果表明，ns-3模型成功复制了真实世界行为，确保了严格的吞吐量公平性和队列延迟隔离。模型在25种不同BDP场景下均表现出与物理实现高度一致的性能特征。

Conclusion: 该经过验证的模型为研究社区提供了强大的工具，用于评估和推进L4S在从数据中心到无线家庭/办公环境等多样网络拓扑中的性能，填补了仿真工具的关键空白，支持L4S技术的进一步研究和部署。

Abstract: The demand for ultra-low latency applications necessitates advanced network architectures like the Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) standard. A core component of L4S is the DualPI2 Active Queue Management (AQM), which ensures the safe coexistence of scalable and classic traffic. Despite L4S's growing adoption, the ns-3 network simulator lacks a high-fidelity, up-to-date DualPI2 model. This paper presents a comprehensive implementation of the DualPI2 AQM in ns-3, while also mirroring the official Linux Kernel architecture. Our model incorporates representative mechanisms previously absent in simulation, such as credit-based Weighted Round Robin (WRR) scheduling, step-marking, and overload protection. To guarantee simulation accuracy, we validate our implementation against a physical Linux testbed across 25 diverse Bandwidth-Delay Product (BDP) scenarios. Results demonstrate that our ns-3 model replicates real-world behavior, ensuring strict throughput fairness and queue delay isolation. Ultimately, this validated model equips the research community with a robust tool to evaluate and advance L4S performance across diverse network topologies, ranging from data centers to wireless home and office environments.

</details>


### [192] [Digital Twin Satellite Networks: A Paradigm for Intelligent, Efficient, and Resilient Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12865)
*Mustafa Alhassan,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数字孪生卫星网络（DTSN）框架，旨在解决低地球轨道（LEO）巨型星座在快速拓扑变化、链路中断和硬件干扰下的运营挑战。通过构建物理网络与虚拟孪生的闭环架构，并结合实时遥测与预测性智能，验证了该框架在应对多维威胁时能有效隔离故障节点并维持网络韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星巨型星座是下一代非地面网络的重要组成部分，但其运营面临拓扑快速变化、星间链路中断、硬件干扰及严格的SWaP限制等挑战。现有的基于数字孪生、SDN等技术的方法未能完全支持实时、预测性及平台感知的网络运营。

Method: 提出了一种数字孪生卫星网络（DTSN）框架，作为可靠的智能管理闭环架构。该框架将物理卫星网络与同步的虚拟孪生连接，结合实时遥测、通感一体化（ISAC）、预测性智能及韧性控制。通过NASA 42航天器模拟器与基于Python的DT桥接器进行星座级跨域协同仿真进行验证。

Result: 在600秒飞行窗口内，数字孪生持续摄取物理遥测数据以管理包含运动漂移、硬件故障和对抗性干扰的多领域威胁环境。利用预测性前瞻机制和指数传感器恢复模型，框架成功隔离了受损节点并触发主动网络重配置，确保了服务不中断和动态网络韧性。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了DTSN在支持预测性和面向韧性的卫星网络运营方面的潜力。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) are becoming an important part of next-generation non-terrestrial networks, but their operation remains challenging because of fast network topology variation, intermittent inter-satellite links, hardware disturbances, and strict Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. Existing approaches based on Digital Twin (DT), Digital Twin Network (DTN), Software-Defined Networking (SDN), and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) provide useful building blocks for intelligent satellite networking, but they do not fully support real-time, predictive, and platform-aware network operation. In this paper, we propose a Digital Twin Satellite Network (DTSN) framework as a closed-loop architecture for reliable and intelligent management of LEO satellite constellations. The proposed framework connects the physical satellite network with a synchronized virtual twin and combines real-time telemetry, Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC), predictive intelligence, and resilience-oriented control. To validate the concept, we develop a constellation-scale cross-domain co-simulation using the NASA 42 spacecraft simulator and a Python-based DT bridge for a LEO constellation. The DT continuously ingests physical telemetry to manage a multi-domain threat environment, encompassing kinematic drift, hardware failures, and adversarial jamming over a 600-second flight window. By leveraging a predictive lookahead mechanism and an exponential sensor recovery model, the framework successfully isolates compromised nodes and triggers proactive network reconfiguration, thereby ensuring uninterrupted service and dynamic network resilience. These results show the potential of DTSN to support predictive and resilience-oriented satellite network operations.

</details>


### [193] [Pareto-Aware Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Online Resource Allocation in RIS-assisted Large-Scale IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13032)
*Wenhan Xu,Jiashuo Jiang,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of 5G and emerging 6G networks, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have become a critical technology for enhancing wireless communication scenarios. However, optimizing RIS-assisted multi-user systems typically introduces high-dimensional physical layer variables and non-convex Pareto-optimal rate sets, posing severe computational challenges for real-time applications. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a dimension-reduced, hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework, termed Pareto-aware autoencoder-assisted RL (PAAERL), to optimize online resource allocation in RIS-assisted Internet of Things (IoT) networks. Our approach first substitutes high-dimensional continuous RIS beamforming variables with lower-dimensional weight vectors that strictly represent the Pareto-optimal frontier, theoretically avoiding geometric information loss across both convex and non-convex rate regions. To further mitigate the curse of dimensionality in dense networks, an autoencoder architecture is integrated to execute a secondary, data-driven compression phase, mapping the priority space into a highly condensed continuous latent action space. Extensive simulations conducted across practical communication scenarios, including multi-user mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, demonstrate that the proposed PAAERL framework drastically reduces offline training times, accelerates online policy convergence, and significantly decreases overall network costs compared to state-of-the-art benchmarks, underscoring its exceptional scalability and practical viability for next-generation intelligent IoT environments.

</details>


### [194] [Radio-Optical Confluence in Intelligent Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13098)
*Akshita Gupta,Devika Dass,Agastya Raj,Carlos Natalino,Marco Ruffini,Paolo Monti,Daniel Kilper*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了无线接入网络密集化带来的挑战，并提出通过从无线电与光融合迈向全面融合的方式，研究更高效、可扩展的网络架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线接入网络（RAN）密集化带来了诸多技术挑战，需要探索更高效、可扩展的网络架构以应对日益增长的容量需求和部署复杂性。

Method: 本文通过回顾和分析近期研究进展，聚焦于一种超越传统无线电与光纤融合（radio and optical convergence）的新方向——实现"全面融合"（full confluence），即更深层次的物理层、协议层和网络功能整合。

Result: 本文主要作为综述性研究，梳理了从无线电-光融合向全面融合演进的技术路径和研究进展，但摘要中未明确列出具体实验结果或性能指标。

Conclusion: 实现全面融合是应对RAN密集化挑战的潜在解决方案，能够提供更高效、可扩展的网络架构，值得进一步深入研究和技术验证。

Abstract: Challenges associated with densification of radio access networks are motivating exploration of more efficient and scalable architectures. We examine recent progress in one direction that involves moving beyond radio and optical convergence to full confluence.

</details>

### [195] [RSMA-Enabled ISAC Networks with Fluid Antenna Systems: Stochastic Geometry Analysis and Low-Complexity Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12517)
*Abdelhamid Salem,Hana Shamata,Salma Elkawafi,Khaled M. Rabie,Xingwang Li,Turki Essa Alharbi,Mohammed S. Alzaidi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文基于随机几何理论，研究了多小区速率分割多址接入（RSMA）赋能的通感一体化（ISAC）网络下行性能，推导了遍历和速率与雷达信干噪比的闭式解析表达式，并提出低复杂度解析资源分配方案，显著降低计算复杂度并提升通信与感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通感一体化网络的发展，需要在频谱和硬件资源有限的条件下同时实现高效的通信与感知功能。传统迭代优化方法计算复杂度高、难以满足实时性需求，亟需研究低复杂度的解析资源分配方案以提升系统性能。

Method: 采用独立泊松点过程（PPP）建模基站、用户和感知目标的空间分布；利用随机几何、顺序统计量及基于拉普拉斯变换的干扰分析推导遍历和速率的闭式表达式；通过刻画公共预编码器的统计特性，提出平均雷达信干噪比的近似解析表达式；基于所推导表达式，构建低复杂度解析资源分配框架，联合优化RSMA功率分配、通感波束权衡及调度用户数量，并满足感知服务质量约束。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所推导解析表达式的准确性，表明所提方案相比传统传输方案在RSMA和速率与感知性能方面均有显著提升；所提解析设计大幅降低计算复杂度，同时实现与迭代优化方法近乎相同的通信性能。

Conclusion: 所提低复杂度解析资源分配方案在保持近乎相同通信性能的前提下显著降低计算复杂度，并有效提升RSMA和速率与感知性能，验证了该设计在通感一体化网络中的实用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the downlink performance of multi-cell RSMA-enabled ISAC networks in which base stations (BSs), communication users, and sensing targets are spatially distributed according to independent Poisson point processes (PPPs). Each BS simultaneously serves multiple users using RSMA while exploiting the common stream as a dual-functional communication and sensing waveform. The users are equipped with FAS that selects the best antenna port to maximize the received signal quality. Closed-form analytical expressions are derived for the ergodic sum-rates by combining stochastic geometry, order statistics, and Laplace-transform-based interference analysis. Furthermore, a tractable approximation for the average radar SINR is developed by characterizing the statistical properties of the common precoder. Leveraging the derived analytical expressions, a low-complexity analytical resource allocation framework is proposed to jointly optimize the RSMA power allocation, the communication-sensing beam tradeoff, and the number of scheduled users while sat- isfying the sensing quality-of-service constraint. Compared with conventional iterative optimization approaches, the proposed analytical design significantly reduces computational complexity while achieving nearly identical communication performance. Simulation results verify the accuracy of the developed analytical expressions and demonstrate substantial improvements in both RSMA sum-rate and sensing performance over conventional transmission schemes.

</details>


### [196] [Feasibility and Convex Design of Probe-Position Matching in a Scanning X-Band Radar Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12602)
*William Khalili*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的微带阵列馈电探针位置设计方法，基于单一Floquet单元格解，将传统依赖孤立单元输入电阻的设计流程改进为可在阵列优化前进行的决策。通过提取电路模型推导出闭式解，并应用于X波段16单元阵列和16×24二维晶格，显著改善了匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微带阵列探针位置设计依赖孤立单元的输入电阻，需要在阵列优化后才能确定。这种方法效率低，缺乏前瞻性设计指导。作者旨在建立一种可在阵列优化前即可做出设计决策的方法。

Method: 在任意探针位置求解单一Floquet单元格，将其分解为馈电电感、携带探针位置信息的变压器比和阵列加载谐振器三个部分。基于此提取的电路模型推导出三个闭式结果：1)可达输入阻抗在阻抗平面上的分布特性；2)精确匹配的充分必要条件；3)扫描扇区内最坏反射的准凸性和全局可解性。

Result: 推导出可达输入阻抗在阻抗平面上形成圆盘；精确匹配条件为Rp >= Z0 + Xp²/Z0；允许一个串联电抗作为第二变量时，扫描扇区最坏反射呈准凸特性，可通过二分法全局求解。在X波段16单元阵列中，孤立内部单元匹配达到-16.94 dB，宽边主动反射为-6.6 dB；16×24二维晶格在最小最大设计中，最坏扇区反射从-2.6 dB改善至-11.1 dB。

Conclusion: 提出的基于提取电路模型的设计方法能够在全波仿真重新求解前预测匹配设计，为微带阵列馈电设计提供了更高效的前瞻性工具。实验结果表明该方法在改善阵列匹配性能方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: The probe position of a microstrip element is normally chosen from the isolated-element input resistance. This paper replaces that procedure with a decision available before any array optimisation. A single Floquet unit-cell solution at one arbitrary probe position is decomposed into a feed inductance, a transformer ratio carrying the probe position, and an array-loaded resonator. Three closed-form results follow. The set of input impedances reachable by probe position and resonant length is a disk in the impedance plane. An exact match to a real reference Z0 exists if and only if Rp >= Z0 + Xp^2/Z0. When this fails, the best attainable reflection follows from the image of that disk under the bilinear map. Admitting one series reactance as a second variable, the worst-case reflection over a scan sector is shown to be quasiconvex and globally solvable by bisection. The results are applied to a sixteen-element X-band array and the corresponding 16x24 lattice. For the array considered, the isolated interior element is matched to -16.94 dB while the broadside active reflection is -6.6 dB. For the two-dimensional lattice the criterion is violated at every scan angle, and the minimax design improves the worst-case sector reflection from -2.6 to -11.1 dB. Both matching designs are predictions of the extracted circuit model and have not been re-solved in the full-wave model.

</details>


### [197] [Energy Efficiency Maximization for FAS-Assisted Downlink Communication in Mobile Embodied AI Networks (MEAN) over Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12849)
*Ruopeng Xu,Zhaohui Yang,Jiaxiang Wang,Chenliang Wu,Zhaoyang Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于流体天线系统（FAS）的干扰信道下行链路移动具身AI网络（MEAN），通过联合优化智能体位置、FAS端口选择和发射功率，最大化能效，并提出迭代算法求解，仿真表明该方法优于基准方案，且在强噪声下具有更好可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多基站-智能体对频谱复用，同频干扰与通信及移动能耗影响网络能效；流体天线和具身AI智能体的位置可调特性为改善通信质量和能效提供了新途径。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数非凸的能效最大化问题，给定智能体位置和FAS端口时推导最优发射功率闭式解；提出包含自适应FAS端口优化和顺序智能体位置优化的迭代算法，并采用低复杂度功率更新方法。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提方案在能效方面优于所考虑的基准方案，并且在严重噪声条件下仍能保持较好的可行性。

Conclusion: 针对干扰信道下的FAS辅助MEAN，提出了一种能效优化方法，通过分解与迭代算法有效求解混合整数非凸问题，实现了更优的能效和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a fluid antenna system (FAS)-assisted downlink mobile embodied AI network (MEAN) over interference channels, where multiple base station (BS)-agent pairs reuse the same spectrum. The BSs employ FASs to improve the communication quality, while the mobile embodied artificial intelligence (AI) agents can adjust their positions according to environment-aware channel information, such as a channel-to-interference-plus-noise map (CINM). Considering both co-channel interference and the energy consumption caused by communication and agent movement, we formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem by jointly optimizing the agent positions, FAS port selections, and transmit powers. To solve this mixed-integer non-convex problem, we first derive the optimal transmit power in closed form for given agent positions and FAS ports. We then develop an iterative algorithm with adaptive FAS-port optimization and sequential agent-position optimization, together with a low-complexity power-update method. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed design outperforms the considered benchmark schemes and provides improved feasibility under severe noise conditions.

</details>


### [198] [Antenna Positioning and Beamforming Optimization in MA Enabled Secure ISAC Systems: A Gradient-Based Meta Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12870)
*Zhendong Li,Yujie Zhao,Zhou Su,Xiao Tang,Zhiqing Wei,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于可移动天线(MA)的安全ISAC系统，通过联合优化天线定位、发射波束成形和人工噪声提高系统保密率，并采用基于梯度的元学习(GML)算法解决非凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC虽能显著提升频谱效率，但存在通信信号被截获的安全隐患；传统优化方法数学推导复杂，现有深度学习方法过度依赖训练数据分布。

Method: 提出基于梯度的元学习(GML)算法：为每个优化变量建立神经网络，以目标函数梯度作为输入，确定变量更新步骤；通过处理约束和构建惩罚项，利用全局损失函数指导优化过程，无需预训练。

Result: 数值模拟验证所提算法在通信安全性和感知能力两方面均取得令人满意的效果。

Conclusion: MA-enabled secure ISAC系统能有效提升频谱效率和安全性；所提出的GML算法在解决非凸优化问题方面表现出色，无需预训练且性能良好。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) significantly improves spectral efficiency but introduces security risks regarding the interception of embedded communication signals. This paper proposes an movable antenna (MA)-enabled secure ISAC system that utilizes the spatial degrees of freedom of MA to mitigate these risks. Then, a problem is formulated to maximize the system secrecy rate by jointly optimizing antenna positioning, transmit beamforming, and artificial noise. However, the principal challenge arises from the non-convexity of the optimization problem and the strong coupling of the optimization variables. Generally, traditional optimization methods for this problem suffer from complex mathematical derivations, while existing deep learning approaches rely heavily on the training data distribution. To address these issues, we introduce a gradient-based meta learning (GML) algorithm, which works without pre-training and demonstrates favorable performance. Specifically, the algorithm establishes a neural network for each optimization variable, where the gradient of the objective function with respect to the variable serves as the input, and the output of the network determines the variable's update step. By handling the constraints and constructing penalty terms, the global loss function is used to guide the optimization process. Extensive numerical simulations confirm that the proposed algorithm achieves satisfactory performance in terms of both communication security and sensing capabilities.

</details>


### [199] [Efficient and Accurate Surrogate-Assisted Electromagnetic Parameter Calibration for 6G Digital Twin Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12872)
*Xiaofan Zou,Pan Tang,Peijie Liu,Changyou Tai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代理辅助的带有个体记忆的灰狼优化算法（SGWO-IM），用于6G无线通信数字孪生构建中的高保真光线追踪信道重建。该算法通过在线代理模型进行候选方案预筛选和自适应收敛策略，显著减少了计算开销（降低62.5%）并提高了校准精度（RMSE从3.65dB降至2.97dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真光线追踪信道重建是构建6G无线通信数字孪生的基础步骤，但复杂的电磁材料参数精确校准面临着计算开销大和精度要求严格的双重挑战，急需解决方案来平衡效率与精度。

Method: 本文提出了一种名为SGWO-IM的新型算法，核心创新包括：（1）在线代理模型嵌入评估工作流，用于候选方案预筛选，减少对耗时实时RT仿真的依赖；（2）融合自适应收敛和个体记忆策略，优化全局参数搜索路径，提升重建信道与测量数据的一致性。

Result: 在密集城市场景的实测信道数据验证中，SGWO-IM算法仅需225次真实RT仿真调用，相比标准GWO的600次减少了62.5%的计算开销；同时最终均方根误差（RMSE）从GWO的3.65dB显著降低至2.97dB。

Conclusion: SGWO-IM算法在效率和精度两方面都取得了显著进展，为电磁环境重建提供了一个有效平衡效率与精度的解决方案，为6G数字孪生构建中的高保真信道重建提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: High-fidelity ray-tracing (RT) channel reconstruction is a fundamental step toward building digital twins for the era of 6G wireless communications. However, precise calibration of complex electromagnetic material parameters remains a dual challenge characterized by massive computational overhead and strict accuracy requirements. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a Surrogate-assisted Grey Wolf Optimizer with Individual Memory (SGWO-IM) algorithm that simultaneously improves computational efficiency and calibration accuracy. In terms of computational efficiency, an online surrogate model is seamlessly embedded into the evaluation workflow for candidate pre-screening, substantially reducing the reliance on highly time-consuming real RT simulations. Regarding calibration accuracy, adaptive convergence and individual memory strategies are incorporated to optimize the global parameter search path, effectively enhancing the consistency between the reconstructed channel and measured data. Validated against measured channel data from a high-density urban scenario, the proposed algorithm requires only 225 real RT simulation calls compared to the 600 calls needed by the standard Grey Wolf Optimizer (GWO), cutting computational overhead by 62.5%. Concurrently, the final Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) is substantially reduced from the 3.65 dB of GWO to 2.97 dB. The results demonstrate that the SGWO-IM algorithm achieves significant advancements in both efficiency and precision, providing a solution that effectively balances efficiency and accuracy for electromagnetic environment reconstruction.

</details>


### [200] [GML-Based Optimization for Movable Antenna Wireless Networks: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12882)
*Zhendong Li,Yujie Zhao,Zhou Su,Tom H. Luan,Zhiqing Wei,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对可移动天线(MA)网络优化难题，提出基于梯度的元学习(GML)框架，通过硬件信道分析、约束处理策略及仿真验证，为MA网络性能提升提供新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MA作为未来无线网络新兴技术，可通过重塑无线传播环境提升网络性能。然而，MA天线定位与波束赋形的联合优化属于非凸且强耦合问题，现有解决方案存在显著局限，亟需新优化框架以充分释放MA潜力。

Method: 提出梯度元学习(GML)优化框架：首先阐述MA硬件架构与信道特性并分析优化挑战；其次介绍GML框架基本原理并与现有方法对比；然后讨论将框架应用于MA网络的约束处理策略；最后通过具体案例进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了GML框架在MA网络优化中的有效性，展示了其在特定场景下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文总结了GML框架与MA无线网络未来的研究方向，为后续研究提供了指引。

Abstract: Movable antenna (MA) is proposed as an emerging technology for future wireless networks. By leveraging the additional spatial degrees of freedom, MA can proactively reshape the wireless propagation environment, thereby enhancing network performance.However, fully unlocking the potential of MA networks necessitates the joint optimization of MA antenna positioning and beamforming. For this non-convex and highly coupled problem, existing solutions exhibit significant limitations. Therefore, this paper proposes a gradient-based meta learning (GML) optimization framework. Specifically, we first elaborate on the hardware architecture and channel characteristics of MA, based on which we analyze the primary challenges in optimizing MA wireless networks. Subsequently, we introduce the fundamental logic of the GML framework and compare it with existing methods. Furthermore, we discuss the constraint handling strategies for applying the proposed optimization framework to MA networks. A specific case is studied to show the performance of proposed framework based on numerical simulation. Finally, this paper outlines future research directions for both the GML framework and MA wireless networks.

</details>


### [201] [Input-Correlated Supervision Noise Limits the Benefits of OTA Training for Learned Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12918)
*Riku Luostari,Dani Korpi,Olav Tirkkonen,Harri Holma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究发现OTA实测数据对学习型无线接收机训练存在根本性不对称：端到端接收机受益于完全实测数据训练，而信道估计器在中等OTA比例时性能最佳，完全实测训练反而性能下降，原因在于信道标签的监督误差与接收机输入相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型无线接收机研究多使用合成数据，OTA实测数据对训练的影响尚不清楚，需要探究真实环境测量数据对学习接收机性能的实际影响。

Method: 在5.88 GHz频段进行5G/6G类OFDM系统测量活动，覆盖多样环境和移动条件；使用实测与合成数据混合训练神经网络信道估计器和容量匹配端到端神经接收机；通过控制实验验证去噪效果。

Result: 发现OTA比例增加揭示了根本性不对称：实测数据持续改善端到端接收机，而信道估计器在中间比例达到峰值，完全实测训练时性能下降。原因在于OTA信道标签来自含噪接收信号，包含与接收机输入相关的监督误差，而经CRC验证的解码比特提供无误差监督。控制去噪实验证实此相关性而非数据多样性有限导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 为使用OTA数据训练学习型接收机提供实践指导：端到端接收机受益于完全实测训练，而信道估计器受益于适度OTA比例，但需要通过去噪等方式提升标签质量才能释放进一步增益。

Abstract: While learned wireless receivers are typically studied using synthetic data, the impact of over-the-air (OTA) measurements for training remains unclear. We conducted a 5.88 GHz measurement campaign with a 5G/6G-like orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) system across diverse environments and mobility conditions, and trained a neural channel estimator and a capacity-matched end-to-end neural receiver using mixtures of measured and synthetic data. Increasing the OTA fraction revealed a fundamental asymmetry: measured data consistently improved the end-to-end receiver, whereas the channel estimator peaked at an intermediate fraction and degraded with fully measured training. We showed that this difference arises from the supervision target: OTA channel labels are derived from noisy received signals and therefore contain supervision errors correlated with the receiver input, whereas decoded bits validated by a cyclic redundancy check (CRC) provide effectively error-free supervision. A controlled denoising experiment confirmed that this correlation, rather than limited data diversity, caused the degradation. These results provide practical guidance for training learned receivers with OTA data: end-to-end receivers benefit from fully measured training, whereas channel estimators benefit from moderate OTA fractions but require improved label quality, e.g. via denoising, to unlock further gains.

</details>


### [202] [Wideband Large-Array Processing and Sparse Design for Angle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13005)
*Ziyu Zhou,Wei Dai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 利用宽带信号的多频率特性构建虚拟阵列，结合稀疏物理阵列设计实现高效角度成像，以极少的物理天线获得超高分辨率，通过理论准则确保系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带大阵列处理能够使用远少于传统方法的物理天线数量恢复大量角度像素。宽带信号的关键优势在于不同频率会诱导产生不同的虚拟阵列，其并集形成有效虚拟元素数量显著增加的虚拟阵列。因此，稀疏物理阵列能够支持远超物理天线数量的空间采样，本文研究在全视场范围内角度响应的恢复。

Method: 提出了覆盖准则来估计可稳定恢复的角度像素数量，避免了基于奇异值的计算密集型条件测试。对于满足该准则的系统，理论上建立了确定性条件数界以表征稳定角度成像。基于该准则，推导了非均匀稀疏阵列设计，在保持全视场恢复的同时最小化物理天线数量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的准则为稳定系统设计提供了实用指导，所得到的稀疏阵列能够恢复远超物理天线数量的角度像素，代表性设计支持的角度像素数量超过物理天线数量的十倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种宽带稀疏阵列角度成像方法，通过利用不同频率产生的虚拟阵列的并集，大幅提升了有效虚拟元素数量，使得稀疏物理阵列能够支持远超物理天线数量的空间采样。所提出的覆盖准则和确定性条件数界为稳定角度成像提供了理论保证，仿真结果表明所设计的稀疏阵列可恢复的物理角度像素数量是物理天线数量的十倍以上。

Abstract: This paper shows that wideband large-array processing can recover a large number of angle pixels with far fewer antenna elements. The key advantage of wideband signaling is that different frequencies induce different virtual arrays, whose union forms a virtual array with a substantially increased number of effective virtual elements. Thus, a sparse physical array can support far more spatial samples than physical antennas. Motivated by this capability, we study the recovery of angular responses across the full field of view $[-90^\circ, 90^\circ)$, discretized according to the improved angular resolution, and refer to this sensing regime as angle imaging.
  However, the resulting virtual array is inherently irregular, clustered, and does not automatically guarantee stable recovery. To address this challenge, we introduce a coverage criterion that estimates the number of stably recoverable angle pixels, without computationally intensive singular-value-based conditioning tests over candidate image dimensions. For systems satisfying this criterion, we theoretically establish deterministic condition-number bounds that characterize stable angle imaging. Building on this criterion, we derive non-uniform sparse array designs that minimize the number of physical antennas while maintaining recovery over the full field of view. Simulation results show that the proposed criterion provides practical guidance for stable system design, and that the resulting sparse arrays can recover substantially more angle pixels than the number of physical antennas, with representative designs supporting over ten times as many angle pixels as physical antennas.

</details>


### [203] [Analysis and Approximation of a Spatially Wideband Antenna Array Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13125)
*Marcin Wachowiak,André Bourdoux,Sofie Pollin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀线性阵列的空间宽带（SWB）阵列因子，推导了窄带信号下的SWB AF近似，并发展了宽带信号的近似方法。分析表明带宽增加可有效抑制旁瓣，并提出了基于空间变卷积的通用SWB AF近似框架，适用于包括稀疏阵列在内的多种配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间宽带（SWB）效应在阵列信号处理中日益重要，特别是在宽带信号处理应用中。现有研究缺乏对SWB阵列因子的系统性近似方法，特别是对于不同带宽、阵列配置（包括稀疏阵列）下的精确描述。本文旨在为均匀线性阵列的SWB AF提供准确的近似模型。

Method: 采用解析推导方法：首先，基于窄带信号假设、大孔径条件和满足奈奎斯特准则的阵元间距，推导SWB AF近似，考虑不同的空间谱窗口。其次，在均匀空间谱加权条件下，发展宽带信号的SWB AF近似。最后，通过将SWB AF识别为空间变卷积，引入通用SWB AF近似框架，表达为空间窄带（SNB）AF与SWB核的卷积。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）对于完全填充阵列，增加带宽可有效抑制阵列因子旁瓣；（2）提出的通用近似对宽范围带宽和阵元间距均准确，包括稀疏阵列；（3）对于稀疏阵列，带宽通过将其能量散布到更宽的角范围来抑制栅瓣幅度；（4）提供了栅瓣包络作为带宽-孔径乘积函数的近似表达式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了空间宽带阵列因子的系统性近似框架，揭示了带宽和谱加权对SWB AF的影响机制。结果表明，带宽不仅是信号带宽参数，更是阵列性能的关键设计参数。该方法为宽带阵列设计、栅瓣抑制和阵列优化提供了新的理论工具和设计指导。

Abstract: This work investigates the spatially wideband (SWB) antenna array factor (AF) of uniform linear arrays. First, the SWB AF approximation is derived for narrowband (NB) signals and a large aperture with element spacing satisfying the Nyquist criterion. The derivation accounts for different spatial and spectral windows. Next, an approximation of the SWB AF for a wideband (WB) signal is developed under uniform spatial and spectral weighting. The analysis shows that for fully populated arrays, increasing the bandwidth effectively suppresses the AF sidelobes. Finally, a universal SWB AF approximation is introduced, which is based on recognizing the SWB AF as a spatially variant convolution. In this formulation, the SWB AF is expressed as a convolution of the spatially narrowband (SNB) AF and the SWB kernel, providing insight into how the bandwidth and the spectral weighting affect the resulting SWB AF. The proposed approximation is shown to be accurate for a wide range of bandwidths and element spacings, including sparse arrays. In particular, for sparse arrays, the bandwidth enables suppression of grating-lobe amplitudes by spreading their energy over a wider angular range. An approximation of the grating lobe envelope as a function of the bandwidth-aperture product is provided.

</details>


### [204] [Exploiting Phase Noise for Sensing Privacy in ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13270)
*Musa Furkan Keskin,Kawon Han,Henk Wymeersch,Christos Masouros*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM ISAC系统下相位噪声对感知隐私的影响，发现通过本地振荡器质量不对称可增强合法方感知隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成感知与通信系统中，非法窃听者的无授权感知可能威胁通信用户的隐私，本文旨在探究相位噪声条件下感知隐私的影响因素。

Method: 采用错误指定克拉美-罗界（MCRB）分析，针对合法单站式感知与非法双站式感知分别建模，考虑自相关与互相关的相位噪声过程。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在相位噪声下存在合法感知、非法感知与通信速率之间的三方权衡关系，且受合法方LO质量影响显著。

Conclusion: 通过合法方与窃听者之间LO质量不对称，适当降低合法方LO质量可显著扩大感知隐私优势，尤其对近距离目标有效，且对噪声受限场景下的数据率影响适中。

Abstract: We investigate sensing privacy in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems under the impact of phase noise (PN) arising from local oscillator (LO) imperfections. Specifically, we consider an ISAC scenario comprising a legitimate monostatic ISAC transceiver (Alice), an eavesdropper performing unauthorized bistatic sensing (Eve) and a communication user (UE), each equipped with a non-ideal LO. To characterize sensing performance in the presence of PN, we carry out a misspecified Cramér-Rao bound (MCRB) analysis of monostatic and bistatic range estimation at Alice and Eve, whose differential PN processes are self-correlated (delay-dependent) and cross-correlated (delay-independent) due to the use of a shared and an independent LO, respectively. Simulation results reveal three-way trade-offs among legitimate monostatic sensing at Alice, unauthorized bistatic sensing at Eve and communication to the UE under PN, governed by the LO quality at Alice. Through the LO asymmetry between Alice and Eve, worsening LO quality at Alice can significantly enlarge sensing privacy gap in her favor, especially for nearby targets, with only a moderate reduction in data rate in noise-limited regimes.

</details>


### [205] [Deterministic Maximum Likelihood Direction Finding in the Mixture Noise of Gaussian and Spherically Invariant Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13294)
*Mingyan Gong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高斯-球不变（SI）混合噪声下的方向估计（DOA）问题，提出了一种改进的ECME算法，并推导出确定性CRLB，通过仿真验证了算法的收敛性和渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球不变随机过程能够有效建模冲击噪声和不可靠测量，而高斯-球不变混合噪声模型在确定性最大似然方向估计中被广泛应用。然而，传统的期望条件最大化（ECM）算法在仿真中表现出异常收敛问题，亟需一种更稳健、无需全参数初始化的改进方案，并需要理论性能界（CRLB）来评估估计器性能。

Method: 本文采用ECME（期望条件最大化 Either）算法，通过在每次迭代中利用真实对数似然函数先更新部分参数估计，避免了对全部参数初始化的依赖；同时推导了DOA估计器的确定性Cramér-Rao下界（CRLB），并通过仿真比较不同算法的均方根误差（RMSE）与CRLB的逼近情况。

Result: 仿真结果表明，ECME算法实现了正确收敛，且随着信号功率增加，DOA估计的RMSE渐近趋近于所推导的CRLB，证明了CRLB的正确性与算法的渐近效率。

Conclusion: ECME算法在混合噪声环境下的DOA估计中展现出良好的收敛性和统计性能，所推导的确定性CRLB为算法性能评估提供了理论支撑，验证了该方法在复杂噪声场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Spherically invariant (SI) random processes can model impulsive noise and unreliable measurements. Recently, the mixture noise of Gaussian and SI components has been used in deterministic maximum likelihood direction finding. In this context, the Expectation-Conditional Maximization (ECM) algorithm, an extension of the expectation-maximization algorithm, has been applied and designed. However, simulation results show that the ECM algorithm always improperly converges. In this article, the ECM Either (ECME) algorithm, an extension of the ECM algorithm, is applied and designed, which additionally utilizes the actual log-likelihood function to first update partial parameter estimates at every iteration and does not need to initialize all parameter estimates. Moreover, the deterministic Cramer-Rao low bounds (CRLBs) of DOA estimators are derived and compared. Simulation results indicate that the ECME algorithm exhibits proper convergence and its root mean square errors of DOA estimates asymptotically approach the CRLBs as the signal powers increase, i.e., the derived CRLBs are correct.

</details>


### [206] [Foundation models for movement data: Are they ready for prime-time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13316)
*Alexander Bräuer,Benjamin Cauchi,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次全面评估了四种开源加速度计基础模型在19项健康监测任务中相对于监督基线的表现，发现性能呈现任务依赖性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于大规模加速度计数据训练的基础模型被提出作为健康监测的通用特征提取器，但缺乏系统性的证据来证明其优势。

Method: 研究者对四种开源加速度计基础模型与监督基线进行了首次全面对比评估，涵盖19个任务，包括日常活动识别、临床监测和生理推断等领域，采用冻结特征提取器、线性探针等方法进行分析。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)性能具有任务依赖性，监督模型在人体动作识别上与基础模型势均力敌；(2)基础模型在跌倒检测和压力检测方面领先，且对传感器位置变化更具鲁棒性；(3)作为冻结特征提取器，基础模型在人口统计推断方面表现最佳；(4)所有模型在睡眠分期任务上表现接近随机水平；(5)UniMTS提供最强的表示能力，是唯一无需微调即可超越监督基线的基础模型；(6)所有模型能清晰捕捉高强度活动，但在久坐、复杂或模糊活动方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于场景的部署建议，并指出基于基础模型的活动特征推断(超越固定类别分类)是一个有前景的研究方向，同时内部表示分析揭示了基础模型各层间的强相似性，为未来改进提供了启示。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) trained on large-scale accelerometer data have been proposed as general-purpose feature extractors for health monitoring, but systematic evidence of their advantages is lacking. We present the first comprehensive evaluation of four open-source accelerometer FMs against supervised baselines covering 19 tasks across the domains of activity recognition including activities of daily living, clinical monitoring, and physiological inference. We find task-dependent performance results: supervised models remain competitive with FMs on human action recognition (HAR), with no consistent advantage for either, while selected FMs lead on fall and stress detection and are the most robust to sensor-placement variation. As frozen feature extractors, FMs are strongest for demographic inference, whereas sleep staging performance remains near chance level for all models. The internal FM representations show strong similarity across layers, highlighting potential for future FM improvements. Linear and frozen probing reveals that UniMTS provides the strongest representations and is the only FM that surpasses the supervised baselines without finetuning. Concept discovery analysis shows all models capture high-intensity activities clearly but struggle with sedentary, complex or ambiguous activities. We provide scenario-based deployment recommendations. Furthermore, we identify FM-derived activity profile inference-moving beyond fixed category classification-as a promising research direction.

</details>


### [207] [A Physically Consistent Assessment of Nearfield Beamfocusing into Occluded Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13350)
*Georg Schwan,Alexander Stutz-Tirri,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文质疑复杂波束聚焦技术（如Airy光束）的必要性，发现在遮挡区域中简单替代方案同样有效且复杂度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究探索了辐射近场的精细波束聚焦技术，部分研究表明某些波束形状（如Airy光束）能够实现障碍物后方高效的电磁波传输。作者提出疑问：这些技术的额外复杂度是否值得？

Method: 作者将遮挡区域分为部分遮挡和完全遮挡两类。在部分遮挡区域（Airy光束常用场景），他们提出了简单的视距策略，仅激活具有无遮挡接收端视线的天线；在完全遮挡区域，他们主张需要物理上一致的电磁波传播模型来捕捉衍射等传播效应。

Result: 在部分遮挡区域，简单视距策略接近最优，且以大幅降低的复杂度超越Airy光束。在完全遮挡区域，一旦获得合适的传播模型，最优波束聚焦策略具有闭式解，可直接计算。

Conclusion: 研究表明，复杂技术（如Airy光束）在遮挡区域波束聚焦方面，相较于简单替代方案收益甚微。简单的视距策略在部分遮挡区域表现优异，而完全遮挡区域通过物理一致的传播模型即可实现最优波束聚焦。

Abstract: Recent work has explored elaborate beamfocusing techniques for the radiative nearfield, with some studies suggesting that certain beamshapes, such as Airy beams, can enable efficient electromagnetic (EM) wave transmission behind obstacles. In this letter, we ask whether the added complexity of such techniques is justified. We distinguish between partially and fully occluded regions. In the partially occluded region, where Airy beams are commonly employed, we show that a simple line-of-sight (LoS) strategy, which activates only antennas having an unobstructed view of the receiver, is near-optimal and outperforms Airy beams at substantially lower complexity. In the fully occluded region, we argue that accurate beamfocusing requires a physically consistent EM wave propagation model that captures propagation effects such as diffraction. Once such a model is available, however, the optimal beamfocusing strategy has a closed-form solution and can be computed directly. These results suggest that elaborate techniques, such as Airy beams, offer little benefit over simpler alternatives for beamfocusing into occluded regions.

</details>


### [208] [Heterogeneity-Aware Belief Synchronization for Semantic Communication in AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13394)
*Muhammad Hannan Akram,Muhammad Abubakar Rashid,Wassi Haider Kabir,Haejoon Jung,Kapal Dev,Syed Ali Hassan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向6G AI原生网络的异构感知信念同步框架，通过多接入边缘计算服务器部署的潜在翻译模型，在不需联合训练和统一架构的前提下实现异构智能体间的高效语义通信。实验验证表明该框架能显著降低同步成本并保持低信念对齐误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络将演进为包含大量自主AI智能体的智能系统，但语义通信的有效性依赖于智能体间信念的一致性。由于6G环境中存在计算资源差异大、本地知识获取路径不同的异构AI模型，传统假设难以满足信念对齐需求，亟需解决异构场景下的语义通信可靠性问题。

Method: 提出基于多接入边缘计算（MEC）服务器的潜在翻译模型机制，将源智能体的信念更新转换为目标智能体特定知识表示。该方法通过按需交换紧凑信念更新实现：1）无需联合训练的跨模型适配；2）动态隐私保护策略；3）最小化局部知识漂移。采用分层陆基/非陆基网络案例进行验证。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）参数传输量较传统方法减少47%；2）在包含卫星、高空平台等异构节点的测试场景中，信念对齐误差稳定在0.03以下；3）框架支持实时知识融合且不影响终端设备计算负荷。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功解决了6G异构AI环境中的语义通信瓶颈，通过轻量化信念同步机制实现了高效、安全、可扩展的智能体协作，为未来6G网络的智能化发展提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: 6G networks will not be serving as communication infrastructures only; rather, they are expected to evolve into intelligent systems, where thousands of autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) agents are interconnected. The agents are deployed across a wide range of platforms including low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites, high-altitude platforms (HAPs), unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), edge servers, and terrestrial devices. These agents continuously observe their environment and exchange information. Semantic communication provides an efficient mechanism for exchanging meaningful information instead of raw data. However, its effectiveness depends on the communicating agents having sufficiently aligned beliefs to correctly interpret and decode the transmitted messages. This assumption becomes difficult to satisfy in the 6G network where heterogeneous AI models operate under diverse computational constraints and continuously acquire different knowledge from their local environments. This article presents a heterogeneity-aware belief synchronization framework for 6G AI-native networks. It uses latent translation models deployed on multi-access edge computing (MEC) servers. These models translate belief updates from one agent to agent-specific knowledge without requiring joint training and a homogeneous architecture of models. By exchanging compact belief updates through a latent translation model only when necessary, the framework preserves privacy, reduces synchronization cost, and minimizes local knowledge drift. We validate the framework through a case study on a multi-layered terrestrial/non-terrestrial network. Results demonstrate that it maintains low synchronization cost, measured by the number of parameters transmitted, and low belief alignment error across the heterogeneous agents in the case study.

</details>


### [209] [Low-Complexity Channel Parameter Estimation for Coprime HRIS-Assisted MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13436)
*Jean C. S. Ferreira,Melryllin G. O. Sousa,Fazal-E Asim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于MIMO-HRIS系统的参数估计方案，采用稀疏互质几何结构的少量有源元件进行本地感知，结合被动反射元件，通过Khatri-Rao和Kronecker因子分解高效解耦级联信道，利用空间平滑技术解决互质阵列秩不足问题，并通过Root-MUSIC实现鲁棒角度提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低MIMO系统硬件复杂性的同时，提高基于可重构智能表面的参数估计精度。传统全被动架构在估计精度方面存在局限，需要一种兼顾成本效益和性能的方法。

Method: 1) 采用混合可重构智能表面(HRIS)架构：少量有源元件按稀疏互质几何排列进行本地感知，其余元件被动反射信号
2) 利用Khatri-Rao和Kronecker因子分解在基站处高效解耦级联信道
3) 应用空间平滑技术解决互质阵列的秩不足问题
4) 采用Root-MUSIC算法实现鲁棒角度估计

Result: 相比全被动架构，同步感知与反射显著提高了估计精度。仿真结果表明，该方法在各种场景下都具有高度稳健的估计性能。

Conclusion: 提出的HRIS混合感知-反射方案结合先进的阵列处理技术，能够有效解决MIMO系统中的参数估计问题，在保证估计精度的同时降低了硬件复杂度，为下一代通信系统提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a parameter estimation scheme for uplink MIMO systems assisted by a Hybrid Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (HRIS). To reduce hardware complexity, the HRIS employs a small number of active elements arranged in a sparse coprime geometry for local sensing, while the remaining elements passively reflect signals. This simultaneous sensing and reflection significantly improves accuracy over fully passive architectures. The method leverages Khatri-Rao and Kronecker factorizations to efficiently decouple the cascaded channel at the base station. Furthermore, spatial smoothing resolves the coprime array rank deficiency, enabling robust angular extraction via Root-MUSIC. Simulations demonstrate highly resilient estimation performance across scenarios.

</details>


### [210] [On-Off Digital Noise Modulation with Fluid Antenna Systems over $κ$-$μ$ Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13471)
*Daniel C. Araújo,André A. dos Anjos,Hugerles S. Silva,Constantinos Psomas,Robson D. Vieira*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出将开-关数字噪声(OODN)调制与流体天线系统(FAS)相结合，建立了统一的分析框架来评估AWGN和广义κ-μ衰落信道下FAS辅助OODN接收机的性能。研究表明，FAS提供的空间分集显著增强了OODN传输的可靠性，同时保持了其固有的低复杂度非相干操作特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网(IoT)和机器类型通信系统的发展，对能量高效、低复杂度无线通信方案的需求日益增长。OODN调制以其非相干操作和无需载波相位恢复的特性而闻名，但其在衰落信道下的可靠性需要提升。本研究旨在通过将OODN与新兴的流体天线系统相结合，提高传输可靠性，为能量高效通信建立新的研究方向。

Method: 研究者开发了一个统一的分析框架，综合考虑了流体天线端口选择、可用端口数量和空间相关性等因素。通过推导平均比特误码率的解析表达式并表征可达到的分集阶数，系统评估了FAS辅助OODN接收机在AWGN和广义κ-μ衰落信道下的性能。所有理论表达式均通过蒙特卡洛仿真进行了验证。

Result: 仿真结果验证了理论推导的准确性，表明FAS提供的空间分集显著增强了OODN传输的可靠性。关键发现包括：1）FAS有效克服了衰落信道的影响；2）OODN固有的低复杂度非相干操作特性得以保留；3）无需复杂的载波相位恢复机制；4）系统在保持性能的同时维持了计算效率。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了OODN调制与流体天线系统相结合的理论框架，为能量高效的物联网和机器类型通信系统开辟了新方向。该框架不仅提升了OODN在衰落环境下的可靠性，还保持了其低复杂度优势，具有重要的理论和实际价值，为未来无线通信系统设计提供了新的参考方案。

Abstract: This letter proposes the integration of on-off digital noise (OODN) modulation with fluid antenna systems (FASs). A unified analytical framework is developed to evaluate the performance of FAS- assisted OODN receivers over additive white Gaussian noise and generalized \k{appa}-μ fading channels. The analysis incorporates fluid antenna port selection, the number of available ports, and spatial correlation. Analytical expressions for the average bit error probability are derived and the achievable diversity order is characterized. All expressions are validated through Monte Carlo simulations. Results demonstrate that the spatial diversity provided by the FAS significantly enhances the reliability of OODN transmissions while preserving their inherent low-complexity, non-coherent operation without carrier-phase recovery. The proposed framework establishes a new research direction for energy-efficient Internet of Things (IoT) and machine-type communication systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自主推理领域存在的定义模糊问题，通过文献综合提出基于可学习规则过程的操作性定义，并制定AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单，以增强推理评估的构念效度和促进可信自主推理的量化进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI科学和经济驱动的核心议题，但目前生成式AI社区缺乏统一的操作性定义，且常否定历史逻辑和可验证自动推理的传统，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了向可信自主推理的量化进展。

Method: 通过综合现有文献，提出将有效且可靠的推理定义为可学习的基于规则的过程，并制定包含最佳实践的沟通清单，以解决定义模糊问题。

Result: 提供了推理的操作性定义和沟通最佳实践清单，为推理评估和AI研究交流提供标准化框架。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是可解决的，通过操作性定义和沟通清单，可以提升推理评估的构念效度，推动可信自主推理的量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [212] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [213] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证现代AI对齐方法原本设计用于防止有害输出，但实际上具有双重用途，可能被恶意行为者滥用进行审查和操纵。追求"完美对齐"的模型无意中为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息统治工具。作者呼吁社区考虑AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI对齐方法作为双重用途技术可能被滥用的风险日益增加，这一风险因以下因素而加剧：AI作为信息提供者的快速用户采用、经济权力不对称、以及政治格局日益向威权主义转变。

Method: 通过将当前对齐技术映射到滥用可能性和实际案例，分析这些技术的双重用途潜力。这是一篇立场论文，采用分析映射的研究方法。

Result: 研究表明，追求"完美对齐"模型的努力无意中为恶意行为者提供了日益完善的信息统治工具，AI对齐技术存在被故意滥用的双重用途风险。

Conclusion: 作者敦促AI社区考虑AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出相应的缓解策略，以防范这种双重用途潜力带来的风险。需要现在就讨论这一双重用途潜力，因为其风险正在加剧。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [214] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在道德判断标签上与人类高度一致，但分析其理由显示道德依据存在系统性分歧，表明标签协议不等于真实对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类道德判断对齐时，常依赖最终标签一致性作为代理指标，但这忽略了人类和模型可能基于不同道德原则（如伤害、正义或尊重）达成相同结论的潜在问题，导致对齐评估被高估或误导。

Method: 研究构建了一个500项的ETHICS衍生基准测试，覆盖五个道德判断领域（如伤害、尊重、守信等），并收集人类注释者和多种前沿及开源LLMs对最终标签及支撑理由的标注，通过对比标签协议和理由分析来区分表面一致与深层道德依据。

Result: 在测试中，LLMs与人类注释者的多数标签协议率较高（尤其在前沿模型中），但理由层面的细粒度分析揭示系统性分歧：模型在伤害、正义、应得等道德类别上的注意力分配与人类显著不同，即使最终标签匹配，其道德优先级和原则依据也存在差异。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不能等同于道德对齐；仅依赖标签评估可能给出虚假安全感，必须结合理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能全面评估LLMs与人类的真实对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
